Keep an agent honest across many sessions with `CONTEXT.md`, a spec, `/clear`, `/compact`, and `/handoff`.\n--\n109:    R[\"/research<br/>learn the domain\"] --> G[\"/grill-with-docs<br/>interviewed one question at a time<br/>→ CONTEXT.md + ADRs\"]\n110-    G --> S[\"/to-spec<br/>→ one spec issue\"]\n111-    S --> T[\"/to-tickets<br/>→ 7-9 ticket issues,<br/>each a vertical slice\"]\n112-    T --> I[\"/implement #N<br/>drives /tdd: red → green → refactor\"]\n--\n130:| **T00, already done:** a \"hello admin\" page, one passing test, `cms publish` shipping a placeholder to Pages | Your data model, login, roles |\n131:| Folder layout and test helpers such as `client_as(\"editor\")` | Every route, form, and screen |\n132-| Two example ADRs, a security checklist, `CLAUDE.md`, hooks, pinned skills, `check_submission.py`, README skeleton | Your own ADRs, spec, tickets, tests |\n133-\n134-The template contains **no CMS features**. That is deliberate: features are what you are being taught to produce.\n--\n171:This proves the whole chain works — Python, the app, the templates — before you add anything. If it fails now, fix it now; do not start the grill on a broken setup.\n172-\n173-### 1.4 Configure the skills\n174-\n--\n197:**Expected:** it names `/grill-with-docs → /to-spec → /to-tickets → /implement → /code-review`.\n198-\n199-> **Name clash.** This skill set ships its own `/teach`, which is different from the `/teach` you wrote in Week 4. In this repo, `/teach` means the mattpocock one. If you want yours here, copy it to `.claude/skills/teach-log/SKILL.md` and rename it inside the file. Vetting names before installing is a real skill; this is your first collision.\n200-\n--\n241:5. **Users → Add New**: create a user with the **Editor** role. Log out, log in as them. Try to reach **Users**. Write down exactly what happens.\n242-6. Find where **Posts → All Posts** lets you filter by status. That screen is the content list you will build.\n243-\n244-### 2.2 Write your field notes\n--\n248:These notes are graded (S1.1) and they are the raw material for your grill answers. Write like you are telling a friend, not like you are writing a lab report.\n249-\n250-### 2.3 Get the vocabulary\n251-\n--\n254:/research how content management systems model content, users and roles, and publishing — compare WordPress, Drupal, and static-site generators\n255-```\n256-\n257:Skim the output for terms: *content type, slug, draft/published, role, capability, permalink, taxonomy, theme, headless*. You do not need all of them. You need the handful you will actually use.\n258-\n259----\n260-\n261:## Part 3 — Your client\n262-\n263-**Time: 20 min.**\n264-\n--\n267:### 3.1 Pick a client\n268-\n269-Choose a real or plausible one you know something about: a club you belong to, a lab, a band, a campus office, a family business, a team. You will not contact them; you just need their reality.\n270-\n--\n279:Create `notes/client-brief.md`. Half a page, in your words:\n280-\n281-- Who runs the site, and how comfortable are they with computers?\n282-- Who reads it, and what are they usually trying to find?\n--\n287:### 3.3 The required capabilities\n288-\n289:Your CMS can name things however your client would. Underneath, all seven of these must work. This list is fixed; how you get there is yours.\n290-\n291-1. **Login** — admins and editors sign in and out; passwords are stored hashed, never as text.\n292:2. **Two roles** — *admin* and *editor*, with genuinely different permissions.\n293-3. **Posts** — create, read, update, delete; title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published, author, timestamps.\n294-4. **Pages** — same, and published pages appear in the public navigation.\n295-5. **Admin console** — dashboard with counts, a content list you can filter, an editor with a preview.\n296:6. **User management** — admin only: create users, change roles, deactivate; a deactivated user cannot log in.\n297-7. **Public site and publish** — only published content, Markdown sanitized, nav built from pages, `cms publish` writes `site/` with relative paths.\n298-\n299-**Deliberately excluded** (do not build these): multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders, rich-text WYSIWYG editors. They cost far more than they teach.\n--\n358:cat .claude/state/phase        # expect: grill, spec, tickets, or T03\n359-```\n360-\n361-### 5.4 Write your budget plan (before your first ticket)\n--\n368:| `/grill-with-docs`, `/to-spec`, `/to-tickets` | `opusplan` or Opus | high | |\n369-| `/implement` + `/tdd` (per ticket) | Sonnet | medium | |\n370-| `/code-review` (per ticket) | Sonnet | high | |\n371-\n--\n407:grill             22     31.0%     6.0%  Opus\n408-\n409-Tickets done: 1 · average weekly cost per ticket: 4.0%\n410-Forecast: 6 tickets need ~24% of the weekly cap; 71% remains → FITS\n--\n423:### 6.1 Grill (one session, fresh window)\n424-\n425-```text\n426-/clear\n427:/grill-with-docs I am building a small CMS for <your client, in one sentence>. Here are my field notes: notes/cms-field-notes.md and my brief: notes/client-brief.md. The 7 required capabilities are in the manual; help me decide what they mean for this client.\n428-```\n429-\n430-It asks **one question at a time**, with a recommended answer attached. How to be good at this:\n--\n432:- **Answer with your client's reality**, not with generalities. \"Karen posts weekly and will never remember a slug\" is worth more than \"yes, sounds good.\"\n433-- **Override the recommendation when you disagree**, and say why. That sentence is the single strongest evidence of your own judgment, and factor A samples exactly these answers.\n434-- **Watch `CONTEXT.md` fill up** as terms resolve. Open it in VS Code beside the terminal.\n435-- **Expect few or no ADRs.** They are reserved for decisions that are hard to reverse. Two are already written for you as examples.\n--\n505:If you leave a session for a different tool, directory, or task, use `/handoff`, and copy the file it writes into `docs/handoff/{NN}_{purpose}_{YYYYMMDD}.md`. At least one handoff is required.\n506-\n507-### 6.5 Drift: catching the model inventing things\n508-\n--\n602:1. What did you build, and which decisions in the grill were **yours**?\n603-2. Where did the AI drift or invent something, and what caught it?\n604-3. Which skills, prompts, and resources did you use, and why those?\n605-4. What would you add next, and what did the budget data tell you?\n--\n620:Use this **only** if your forecast in 5.6 says you will run out of Pro usage before Stage 2. {"parentUuid":"3900ab66-4466-454a-9b5f-3d04e37cc9ed","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHSEfLS7qJQiUEoXBTG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've read the client brief, the field notes, both ADRs, and the seven required capabilities. `CONTEXT.md` is still empty and there are no issues yet, so this grill starts from scratch. I've set the phase to `grill`.\n\nHere's the working frame: a CMS for the **Golf & Country Club of Trujillo**. Office staff post the menu, events and news. Members read them on a static site.\n\n## Round 1\n\n❓ **Q1** - **Who is an Editor, and what can they touch?** The brief says someone \"who is only supposed to post news\" must not change the rules or membership pages, or manage users. The required capabilities call for two roles, Admin and Editor. The open question is where Pages fall:\n- (a) Editors manage Posts only. Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users are Admin-only.\n- (b) Editors manage Posts and Pages. Only Users are Admin-only.\n- (c) Editors can edit only the Pages an Admin assigns to them. This adds per-page permissions.\n\n➡️ **(a).** It matches the \"must NOT happen\" list exactly, and it's a real server-side block, like the WordPress Users page you saw. It isn't just a hidden link.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q2** - **What are the menu, events and news in CMS terms?** Members want three things. The CMS must have Posts, which form a feed, and Pages, which appear in the nav.\n- (a) News and events are both Posts. The Menu is a Page.\n- (b) News is Posts. Events are Posts with an extra **event date**, so the site can show \"upcoming events\" sorted and hide past ones. The Menu is a Page.\n- (c) News is Posts. The Menu and an \"Events\" list are both Pages, and staff edit them by hand.\n\n➡️ **(b)** if you can afford one extra field and ticket. Otherwise **(a)**. \"The next events\" is how members actually look at events, and a feed sorted by post date gets that wrong. Keep in mind that under Q1(a), Editors could no longer change the menu, because it would be a Page.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q3** - **Who can publish?** The brief's worst fear is \"publishes an unfinished post or a wrong menu or price by mistake.\"\n- (a) Editors publish their own posts directly. The editor forces a preview and a confirmation step, like the two clicks you saw in WordPress.\n- (b) Editors can only save drafts or **submit for review**. An Admin publishes. This adds a third status.\n- (c) Only Admins can change the status at all.\n\n➡️ **(a).** The office is small, and \"post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone\" is one of their success criteria. A review queue works against that. The preview and confirmation step addresses the mistake risk.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q4** - **\"Published\" vs. \"on the site.\"** Under ADR-001, a post marked published isn't visible to members until someone runs `cms publish`. Staff will confuse these two states. I'd like to name them separately in the glossary: **Published**, a status in the console, and **Live**, meaning it's on the public site after the last export. Who runs `cms publish`: you, or the office?\n\n➡️ Keep two terms. You, the developer, run `cms publish`, and the console shows \"N published changes not yet live.\" The office can't reasonably run a CLI and git deploy.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q5** - **Language.** Should the admin console and public site be in Spanish, English, or both?\n\n➡️ Both the console and the site in **Spanish**, since members and staff in Trujillo work in Spanish. The code and glossary stay in English, with each English term mapped to its Spanish label (Post → Noticia, Page → Página).\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q6** - **Slugs and addresses.** Should slugs be auto-generated from the title and editable? With or without dates? How should accents be handled (\"Menú de la semana\")?\n\n➡️ Generate slugs automatically from the title, strip accents to ASCII (`menu-de-la-semana`), and let staff edit them before first publish. No dates in the URL. Posts and Pages each need unique slugs. Addresses look like `noticias/<slug>.html` and `<slug>.html`.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q7** - **Time zone.** Your field notes caught WordPress dating a post in UTC.\n\n➡️ Store UTC, display **America/Lima** everywhere: console, public site and event dates. The club doesn't need a setting to change it.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q8** - **Deactivated users and their content.** When an Admin deactivates a user, what happens to their posts?\n\n➡️ Their posts stay, published status unchanged, still credited to them as author. The user just can't log in. Users are never deleted, only deactivated, so authorship never breaks. An Admin can't deactivate themselves, so the club never gets locked out.\n\n---\n\nAnswer as many as you like, overriding wherever the club's reality differs. That's the most valuable part. The terms go into `CONTEXT.md` as they're settled."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4050,"cache_read_input_tokens":42726,"output_tokens":2591,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":948},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4050,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2591,"cache_read_input_tokens":42726,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4050,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4050},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"06dda7b1-446a-4d7e-91b2-150d0e0b5b9b","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYHSEEXsexV74UkiwXp1","attributionSkill":"domain-modeling","type":"assistant","uuid":"2000abc4-51f3-4e41-9e3d-55a8683132df","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:29:50.465Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"65ab32f0-e6ea-4eff-a31e-74795aff745a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5c671565-e83e-49d2-8d50-9af742537a4b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"\n\n<pasted_content id=\"b9c2\">\nQ1: a. Editors only do posts. Rules, membership and About are Admin-only Pages, and users are Admin-only. That is my main \"must not happen\".\nQ2: b for events, they need an event date because members ask \"when is the next event\". But I disagree on the menu. It changes every week, so make it a Post (\"Menú de la semana\"), not a Page. That way an Editor can update it and still can't touch the rules pages.\nQ3: a. The office is small and needs to post in a few minutes, but keep the confirmation step so a wrong price is less likely.\nQ4: Keep two terms, Published and Live. I run cms publish, not the office.\nQ5: Public site in Spanish because the members are Peruvian. Console in English to keep it simple. CONTEXT.md maps each English term to its Spanish label.\nQ6: a. Auto-generated slugs from the title, editable before first publish, no dates in the URL, and accents stripped.\nQ7: Accept. Store UTC, show America/Lima.\nQ8: Accept. Posts stay and the user is deactivated, not deleted. An Admin can't deactivate themselves.\n</pasted_content id=\"b9c2\">\n"},"uuid":"70a9ad0f-f8f7-416f-bf55-6811827e83a4","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:30:53.652Z","permissionMode":"auto","origin":{"kind":"human"},"promptSource":"typed","turnOrigin":"human","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":2,"turnIndex":2},"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

I'm writing the settled terms into `CONTEXT.md` now."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":424,"cache_read_input_tokens":49367,"output_tokens":1901,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":845},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":424,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1901,"cache_read_input_tokens":49367,"cache_creation_input_tokens":424,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":424},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"f66953e0-400b-4cbb-9dde-bac893c9ee3c","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYHYyTV8QC6RfRCecVFK","type":"assistant","uuid":"753a40bd-c7ef-4a74-9d2b-fe54e2f65f6f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:31:03.954Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"753a40bd-c7ef-4a74-9d2b-fe54e2f65f6f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHYzCcpKMk2XQLhdzBF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017R2izLNkCs2SShT5QWQCGJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes and publishes Posts, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User credited with writing a Post or Page. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not the navigation. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club on a specific Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event takes place. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content that appears in the public navigation, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nContent whose status says it belongs on the public site. Becomes Live only at the next site export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is actually on the public site, because it was Published at the time of the last site export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":424,"cache_read_input_tokens":49367,"output_tokens":1901,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":845},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":424,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1901,"cache_read_input_tokens":49367,"cache_creation_input_tokens":424,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":424},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_017R2izLNkCs2SShT5QWQCGJ":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes and publishes Posts, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User credited with writing a Post or Page. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not the navigation. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club on a specific Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event takes place. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content that appears in the public navigation, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nContent whose status says it belongs on the public site. Becomes Live only at the next site export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is actually on the public site, because it was Published at the time of the last site export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"f66953e0-400b-4cbb-9dde-bac893c9ee3c","apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfYHYyTV8QC6RfRCecVFK","type":"assistant","uuid":"fdb28eb0-83a7-485d-acd0-5b6e1b7ef04c","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:31:13.246Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"7760e900-0c42-41f3-9586-5e4e820a4905","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:31:13.370Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"update","filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes and publishes Posts, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User credited with writing a Post or Page. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not the navigation. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club on a specific Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event takes place. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content that appears in the public navigation, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nContent whose status says it belongs on the public site. Becomes Live only at the next site export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is actually on the public site, because it was Published at the time of the last site export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":3,"newStart":1,"newLines":68,"lines":["-# CONTEXT.md glossary","+# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS"," ","-(Your /grill-with-docs session fills this in as terms are settled.)","+A small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. 09-29T19:31:21.980Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"8f51cb4f-7ea5-4fc4-83f8-69d961c0e5c6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHaRuUW12B7Hhy6xwYW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## Round 2\n\nYour answers opened these branches.\n\n❓ **Q9** - **\"Publish\" means two things.** Right now \"publish\" is both the status change an Editor makes (Draft → Published) and the `cms publish` command you run (which makes content Live). Staff will say \"I published it, why can't members see it?\" The command name is fixed by the assignment, but the glossary can use a different word for the action:\n- (a) Call the action **Export**: \"Published at the last export.\"\n- (b) Call it **Deploy**.\n- (c) Keep \"publish\" for both and live with the ambiguity.\n\n➡️ **(a) Export.** It describes what happens (the database is rendered into `site/`) and doesn't collide with the status. I used \"site export\" in the Published/Live definitions above as a placeholder.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q10** - **Which Menu is \"current\"?** A new Menu Post goes up every week.\n- (a) The home page shows only the newest Live Menu. Older Menus stay reachable in a Menu archive.\n- (b) Only the newest Menu is exported at all. Older ones stay Published in the console but are left out of the site.\n- (c) The Editor unpublishes last week's Menu by hand.\n\n➡️ **(a).** It's automatic, so nobody has to remember anything, and the brief's fear of a \"wrong menu or price\" is best answered by the stale one never being the headline. An archive also costs nothing extra.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q11** - **\"Upcoming\" on a static site.** \"Past\" is decided when you export, not when a Member visits. If you export on Monday, a Tuesday event is still listed as upcoming on Wednesday.\n- (a) Upcoming means an Event date on or after the export date. You accept that the list goes stale between exports, and every Event shows its date prominently.\n- (b) Add a small script to the public page that hides past events in the browser. That's JavaScript on a no-JS stack.\n- (c) Show all Events in date order with no upcoming/past split.\n\n➡️ **(a).** Keep the site JS-free, as ADR-002 does, and treat \"export often\" as the fix. Put the date first on every Event.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q12** - **What is an Event date: a date, a date and time, or a range?** Tournaments can run over a weekend; a dinner is at 8 pm.\n\n➡️ **A date plus an optional start time, with no end date.** Multi-day tournaments go in the body text. Ranges add validation and display cases that the club hasn't asked for.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q13** - **Whose Posts can an Editor change?** With a shared weekly Menu, whoever is in the office that day updates it.\n- (a) Any Editor can edit, unpublish or delete any Post.\n- (b) Editors edit any Post but delete only their own.\n- (c) Editors touch only their own Posts.\n\n➡️ **(a).** The office works as one team, and (c) would block the Monday person from fixing Friday's menu price. Keep the Author as the original writer. Should Admins be able to do the same? I assume yes.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q14** - **Taking content down.** If a wrong price goes Live, the fix is to unpublish (Published → Draft) and export. Does unpublishing need the same confirmation step as publishing? And does a delete get a confirmation too?\n\n➡️ **Yes to both.** Deleting is permanent (no trash), so it gets a confirmation. Unpublishing gets one too, because it removes something Members rely on.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q15** - **Navigation order and the home page.** Pages appear in the nav, but in what order? And is the home page (menu, next events, latest news) a Page an Admin writes, or is it generated?\n\n➡️ The **home page is generated**, not a Page, and always comes first in the nav. Pages follow in an order the Admin sets with a number on each Page. Should Posts be listed in the nav by kind (News, Events, Menu archive)? I recommend yes: three fixed nav entries that the system provides."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1990,"cache_read_input_tokens":49791,"output_tokens":1325,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":8},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1990,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1325,"cache_read_input_tokens":49791,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1990,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1990},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"1136885f-4450-466d-9dec-0bc8a4d77aaf","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYHaRVRKjFd17vujW89y","type":"assistant","uuid":"ff3f7b07-e3ff-475f-91a3-94815c113e2a","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:31:28.515Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"f157638d-9d37-43cb-ad02-391f397f5309","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"18e7809d-80a4-4030-9936-7d743e5bc7ef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"\n\n<pasted_content id=\"b9c2\">\nQ9: a. Call the action Export, so Published is only the status and Live means it was in the last export.\nQ10: a. The home page shows only the newest Menu, and older Menus go in a Menu archive. Nobody has to remember to unpublish last week's.\nQ11: a. Upcoming means the event date is on or after the export date. No JavaScript, and we export often.\nQ12: Date plus optional start time, but add an optional end date. Golf tournaments here run over a weekend, so \"Sat to Sun\" should be a real field, not just text in the body.\nQ13: a. The home page is generated, Pages are ordered by a number the Admin sets, and there are three fixed nav entries for News, Events and Menu archive.\n\nI think we have enough. Please do these before we move on:\n1. Update CONTEXT.md with every settled term, at least 8 specific to the club, with the Spanish labels.\n2. List anything that is still undecided.\n3. kens":474,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2143,"cache_read_input_tokens":53106,"cache_creation_input_tokens":474,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":474},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"595e8dd7-33e7-4271-bf71-95d15ba39c76","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfYHh4y6UmixeL895pCho","type":"assistant","uuid":"e5d21e3b-3fd1-4ef2-973a-1b2e5ce4e18d","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:32:52.243Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e5d21e3b-3fd1-4ef2-973a-1b2e5ce4e18d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHh5MuKEKC7CJgSKJSY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JBYnvhebtegy5gX54Jhk1i","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes, publishes and fixes any Post, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User who originally wrote a Post or Page. Stays the original writer even when someone else edits it. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not as its own navigation entry. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club, with an Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event happens: a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Upcoming** (Próximos eventos):\nAn Event whose date is on or after the day of the last Export. Decided at Export, not when a Member visits.\n_Avoid_: future, current\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Current menu** (Menú actual):\nThe newest Live Menu. It's the only Menu shown on the Home page.\n\n**Menu archive** (Menús anteriores):\nThe public list of every Live Menu other than the Current menu.\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content with its own navigation entry, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages, and they set the navigation order.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Home page** (Inicio):\nThe generated public front page showing the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Not a Page; nobody writes it.\n_Avoid_: landing page, index page\n\n**Navigation** (Menú de navegación):\nThe public site's top links: Home page first, then the three fixed entries (News, Events, Menu archive), then Pages in Admin-set order.\n_Avoid_: menu (reserved for the restaurant Menu)\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nA status only: content marked as belonging on the public site. Becomes Live at the next Export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Unpublish** (Despublicar):\nReturning Published content to Draft, so it disappears from the public site at the next Export.\n_Avoid_: hide, archive\n\n**Export** (Exportación):\nRendering all Published content into the public site and deploying it. Run by the developer, never by the office.\n_Avoid_: publish, deploy, build\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is on the public site right now, because it was Published at the last Export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":474,"cache_read_input_tokens":53106,"output_tokens":2143,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":681},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":474,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2143,"cache_read_input_tokens":53106,"cache_creation_input_tokens":474,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":474},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01JBYnvhebtegy5gX54Jhk1i":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes and publishes Posts, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User credited with writing a Post or Page. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not the navigation. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club on a specific Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event takes place. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content that appears in the public navigation, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nContent whose status says it belongs on the public site. Becomes Live only at the next site export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is actually on the public site, because it was Published at the time of the last site export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a06f1aa1-0261-4744-b4fc-4e99b6cd03e4","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"3be950c0-9140-4fb1-89ff-cf453f4ecb81","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"18e7809d-80a4-4030-9936-7d743e5bc7ef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013ynuNyLk5isW2L57iB7ecB","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"c83084ce-bfa6-4e6f-9e00-cc37c06b7e8b","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:33:05.904Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"**Navigation** (Menú de navegación):","newString":"**Navigation** (Navegación):","originalFile":"# Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\n\nA small CMS where the club's office staff write news, events and the weekly menu, and members read them on a public site. The console is in English; the public site is in Spanish, so each term below lists its Spanish label.\n\n## People\n\n**Admin** (Administrador):\nA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n\n**Editor** (Editor):\nA staff member who writes, publishes and fixes any Post, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n\n**Author** (Autor):\nThe User who originally wrote a Post or Page. Stays the original writer even when someone else edits it. An attribute of content, not a role.\n\n**User** (Usuario):\nA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n\n**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\nA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin cannot deactivate themselves.\n_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n\n**Member** (Socio):\nA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n_Avoid_: user, reader\n\n## Content\n\n**Post** (Publicación):\nDated content that appears in a feed, not as its own navigation entry. Every Post is exactly one kind: News, Event or Menu.\n_Avoid_: article, entry\n\n**News** (Noticia):\nA Post announcing club news.\n\n**Event** (Evento):\nA Post about something happening at the club, with an Event date.\n\n**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\nWhen an Event happens: a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n\n**Upcoming** (Próximos eventos):\nAn Event whose date is on or after the day of the last Export. Decided at Export, not when a Member visits.\n_Avoid_: future, current\n\n**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\nA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n_Avoid_: menu page\n\n**Current menu** (Menú actual):\nThe newest Live Menu. It's the only Menu shown on the Home page.\n\n**Menu archive** (Menús anteriores):\nThe public list of every Live Menu other than the Current menu.\n\n**Page** (Página):\nStanding content with its own navigation entry, such as rules, membership or about. Only Admins manage Pages, and they set the navigation order.\n_Avoid_: static page\n\n**Home page** (Inicio):\nThe generated public front page showing the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Not a Page; nobody writes it.\n_Avoid_: landing page, index page\n\n**Navigation** (Menú de navegación):\nThe public site's top links: Home page first, then the three fixed entries (News, Events, Menu archive), then Pages in Admin-set order.\n_Avoid_: menu (reserved for the restaurant Menu)\n\n**Slug**:\nThe last part of a piece of content's public address, made from its title with accents stripped. Can be edited only before first publish. Never contains a date.\n_Avoid_: permalink, URL\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Draft** (Borrador):\nContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n\n**Published** (Publicado):\nA status only: content marked as belonging on the public site. Becomes Live at the next Export.\n_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n\n**Unpublish** (Despublicar):\nReturning Published content to Draft, so it disappears from the public site at the next Export.\n_Avoid_: hide, archive\n\n**Export** (Exportación):\nRendering all Published content into the public site and deploying it. Run by the developer, never by the office.\n_Avoid_: publish, deploy, build\n\n**Live** (En línea):\nContent that is on the public site right now, because it was Published at the last Export.\n_Avoid_: published (when you mean visible to Members)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":64,"oldLines":7,"newStart":64,"newLines":7,"lines":[" The generated public front page showing the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Not a Page; nobody writes it."," _Avoid_: landing page, index page"," ","-**Navigation** (Menú de navegación):","+**Navigation** (Navegación):"," The public site's top links: Home page first, then the three fixed entries (News, Events, Menu archive), then Pages in Admin-set order."," _Avoid_: menu (reserved for the restaurant Menu)"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3be950c0-9140-4fb1-89ff-cf453f4ecb81","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"64c066b7-6f34-4ffd-8bda-358920061fe1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHij6okHcrsfZULSwwC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've updated `CONTEXT.md` with 24 terms, each with its Spanish label. Fifteen are specific to the club: Member, News, Event, Event date, Upcoming, Menu, Current menu, Menu archive, Home page, Navigation, Unpublish, Export, Live, Draft and Published. The Navigation label is \"Navegación\" rather than \"Menú de navegación\", so \"menu\" only ever means the restaurant Menu. I didn't write an ADR, because none of these decisions is both hard to reverse and a real trade-off.\n\n## 1. Still undecided\n\nItems marked **(grill)** are domain decisions for you. The rest are spec-level detail that `/to-spec` can settle.\n\n1. **(grill) Upcoming with an end date.** Your Q12 override changes Q11. On Sunday of a Sat–Sun tournament, is it still Upcoming? I recommend the rule be based on the *end date if present, otherwise the start date*, so an event in progress stays listed.\n2. **(grill) Editing something already Live.** Publish, unpublish and delete are confirmed, but a plain edit is not. An Editor fixing a price in a Published Menu changes what goes Live at the next Export without confirming anything. That's the brief's \"wrong price\" risk again. I recommend saving changes to Published content go through the same confirmation step.\n3. **(grill) Is the Author shown to Members?** I recommend no, since the club speaks as one voice.\n4. **(grill) Media.** Images such as event flyers or photos of dishes aren't in the seven required capabilities. I recommend declaring them out of scope. Images can be linked in Markdown only if they're hosted elsewhere.\n5. **(grill) Last Admin.** An Admin can't deactivate themselves. Can they demote themselves to Editor? I recommend no, for the same reason.\n6. Slug collisions: add a `-2` suffix automatically, or refuse? And does \"first publish\" mean ever, even after an Unpublish?\n7. Which dates Members see on News and Menus (created or published), and in what format.\n8. Dashboard counts, content-list filters and the \"N changes not yet Live\" indicator.\n9. How the first Admin is created (seed script vs. CLI command).\n\n## 2. One-line statements\n\n- **Post:** Dated content of one kind (News, Event or Menu) that any Editor or Admin can write, and that Members find through the Home page and three fixed feeds.\n- **Page:** Standing content (rules, membership, about) that only Admins manage, with its own navigation entry in the order the Admin sets.\n- **Who can do what:** Editors create, edit, publish, unpublish and delete any Post. Admins can do all of that, plus manage Pages and Users. Members only read the public site.\n- **Publishing:** Publishing is a confirmed status change from Draft to Published inside the console. Content becomes Live only when the developer runs an Export, and Drafts never leave the database.\n- **Out of scope:** multi-site, page builders, WYSIWYG editors, review/approval workflows, the office running Exports, JavaScript on the public site, member logins, comments, search and (pending item 4) media uploads.\n\nThe frontier isn't empty yet: items 1–5 are still open. Answer those and I'll close them in `CONTEXT.md`. Then you can confirm we've reached a shared understanding before `/to-spec`."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":305,"cache_read_input_tokens":55812,"output_tokens":1161,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":38},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":305,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1161,"cache_read_input_tokens":55812,"cache_creation_input_tokens":305,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":305},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"3675813d-d2bb-47ac-8ea4-4981bc85bc97","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYHiiizP8S9jAFmDm4X5","type":"assistant","uuid":"2c175fb8-7713-464e-b79e-03ea968c125c","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:33:17.890Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"476140cf-b77d-42cc-a582-67ab91781ef6","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"edited_text_file","filename":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","snippet":"8\tA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n9\t_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n10\t\n11\t**Editor** (Editor):\n12\tA staff member who writes, publishes and fixes any Post, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n13\t_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n14\t\n15\t**Author** (Autor):\n16\tThe User who originally wrote a Post or Page. Stays the original writer even when someone else edits it. Visible only in the console, never to Members. An attribute of content, not a role.\n17\t\n18\t**User** (Usuario):\n19\tA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n20\t_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n21\t\n22\t**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\n23\tA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin can't deactivate or demote themselves, so the club always has an Admin.\n24\t_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n25\t\n26\t**Member** (Socio):\n27\tA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n28\t_Avoid_: user, reader\n29\t\n30\t## Content\n31\t\n...\n38\t\n39\t**Event** (Evento):\n40\tA Post about something happening at the club, with an Event date.\n41\t\n42\t**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\n43\tWhen an Event happens: a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n44\t\n45\t**Upcoming** (Próximos eventos):\n46\tAn Event whose end date (or start date, if there's no end date) is on or after the day of the last Export. A weekend tournament stays Upcoming while it is happening. Decided at Export, not when a Member visits.\n47\t_Avoid_: future, current\n48\t\n49\t**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\n50\tA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n51\t_Avoid_: menu page\n52\t\n53\t**Current menu** (Menú actual):\n54\tThe newest Live Menu. It's the only Menu shown on the Home page.\n...\n76\t\n77\t**Draft** (Borrador):\n78\tContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n79\t\n80\t**Published** (Publicado):\n81\tA status only: content marked as belonging on the public site. Becomes Live at the next Export.\n82\t_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n83\t\n84\t**Confirmation** (Confirmación):\n85\tThe explicit second step required before any change to what Members will see: publishing, unpublishing, deleting, or saving edits to Published content.\n86\t_Avoid_: approval, review\n87\t\n88\t**Unpublish** (Despublicar):\n89\tReturning Published content to Draft, so it disappears from the public site at the next Export.\n90\t_Avoid_: hide, archive\n91\t\n92\t**Export** (Exportación):\n93\tRendering all Published content into the public site and deploying it. Run by the developer, never by the office.\n94\t_Avoid_: publish, deploy, build\n95\t"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"1131a8bf-ca1c-458c-915f-4c2af8356859","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:33:52.256Z","rendered":[{"content":"<system-reminder>\nNote: /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md changed on disk since you last read it. That's usually deliberate, so take it as the current state rather than reverting it; if the change looks wrong, say so rather than undoing it yourself — otherwise no need to call it out. Here are the relevant changes (shown with line numbers):\n8\tA staff member who manages Pages and Users, and can do everything an Editor can.\n9\t_Avoid_: superuser, owner\n10\t\n11\t**Editor** (Editor):\n12\tA staff member who writes, publishes and fixes any Post, and nothing else. An Editor can never touch Pages or Users.\n13\t_Avoid_: author (as a role), staff, poster\n14\t\n15\t**Author** (Autor):\n16\tThe User who originally wrote a Post or Page. Stays the original writer even when someone else edits it. Visible only in the console, never to Members. An attribute of content, not a role.\n17\t\n18\t**User** (Usuario):\n19\tA login belonging to one staff member, with exactly one role: Admin or Editor. Users are never deleted, only Deactivated.\n20\t_Avoid_: account, member (members are readers, not Users)\n21\t\n22\t**Deactivated** (Desactivado):\n23\tA User who can no longer log in. Their content and authorship stay untouched. An Admin can't deactivate or demote themselves, so the club always has an Admin.\n24\t_Avoid_: deleted, removed, banned\n25\t\n26\t**Member** (Socio):\n27\tA club member or family member who reads the public site. Members never log in.\n28\t_Avoid_: user, reader\n29\t\n30\t## Content\n31\t\n...\n38\t\n39\t**Event** (Evento):\n40\tA Post about something happening at the club, with an Event date.\n41\t\n42\t**Event date** (Fecha del evento):\n43\tWhen an Event happens: a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. Distinct from when the Post was written or published.\n44\t\n45\t**Upcoming** (Próximos eventos):\n46\tAn Event whose end date (or start date, if there's no end date) is on or after the day of the last Export. A weekend tournament stays Upcoming while it is happening. Decided at Export, not when a Member visits.\n47\t_Avoid_: future, current\n48\t\n49\t**Menu** (Menú de la semana):\n50\tA Post holding the restaurant's menu and prices for one week.\n51\t_Avoid_: menu page\n52\t\n53\t**Current menu** (Menú actual):\n54\tThe newest Live Menu. It's the only Menu shown on the Home page.\n...\n76\t\n77\t**Draft** (Borrador):\n78\tContent that exists only in the console. A Draft never reaches the public site.\n79\t\n80\t**Published** (Publicado):\n81\tA status only: content marked as belonging on the public site. Becomes Live at the next Export.\n82\t_Avoid_: live (when you mean the status)\n83\t\n84\t**Confirmation** (Confirmación):\n85\tThe explicit second step required before any change to what Members will see: publishing, unpublishing, deleting, or saving edits to Published content.\n86\t_Avoid_: approval, review\n87\t\n88\t**Unpublish** (Despublicar):\n89\tReturning Published content to Draft, so it disappears from the public site at the next Export.\n90\t_Avoid_: hide, archive\n91\t\n92\t**Export** (Exportación):\n93\tRendering all Published content into the public site and deploying it. Run by the developer, never by the office.\n94\t_Avoid_: publish, deploy, build\n95\t\n</system-reminder>"}],"session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"346d0777-ef86-431b-bc2e-2996715dc8e5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"352c82c1-db7e-4925-b71d-47b823e7503e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/to-spec\n\nThis skill takes the current conversation context and codebase understanding and produces a spec. Do NOT interview the user; just synthesize what you already know.\n\nThe issue tracker and triage label vocabulary should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n1. Explore the repo to understand the current state of the codebase, if you haven't already. Use the project's domain glossary vocabulary throughout the spec, and respect any ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n2. Sketch out the seams at which you're going to test the feature. Existing seams should be preferred to new ones. Use the highest seam possible. If new seams are needed, propose them at the highest point you can. The fewer seams across the codebase, the better - the ideal number is one.\n\nCheck with the user that these seams match their expectations.\n\n3. Write the spec using the template below, then publish it to the project issue tracker. Apply the `ready-for-agent` triage label - no need for additional triage.\n\n<spec-template>\n\n## Problem Statement\n\nThe problem that the user is facing, from the user's perspective.\n\n## Solution\n\nThe solution to the problem, from the user's perspective.\n\n## User Stories\n\nA LONG, numbered list of user stories. Each user story should be in the format of:\n\n1. As an <actor>, I want a <feature>, so that <benefit>\n\n<user-story-example>\n1. As a mobile bank customer, I want to see balance on my accounts, so that I can make better informed decisions about my spending\n</user-story-example>\n\nThis list of user stories should be extremely extensive and cover all aspects of the feature.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\nA list of implementation decisions that were made. This can include:\n\n- The modules that will be built/modified\n- The interfaces of those modules that will be modified\n- Technical clarifications from the developer\n- Architectural decisions\n- Schema changes\n- API contracts\n- Specific interactions\n\nDo NOT include specific file paths or code snippets. They may end up being outdated very quickly.\n\nException: if a prototype produced a snippet that encodes a decision more precisely than prose can (state machine, reducer, schema, type shape), inline it within the relevant decision and note briefly that it came from a prototype. Trim to the decision-rich parts, not a working demo, just the important bits.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\nA list of testing decisions that were made. Include:\n\n- A description of what makes a good test (only test external behavior, not implementation details)\n- Which modules will be tested\n- Prior art for the tests (i.e. similar types of tests in the codebase)\n\n## Out of Scope\n\nA description of the things that are out of scope for this spec.\n\n## Further Notes\n\nAny further notes about the feature.\n\n</spec-template>\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"a761bf8f-d14e-4f39-beca-1c0e40c197aa","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:35:12.677Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"6611344d-1a4c-43bc-8f36-1f73d29cbc50","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"352c82c1-db7e-4925-b71d-47b823e7503e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BVXc698d11pA1D69MuB4BS","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\ntemplates:\nadmin\nbase.html\npublic\n\nscripts:\n__pycache__\ncheck_submission.py\nseed_demo.py\nusage_report.py\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.\n- **Comment on an issue**: `gh issue comment <number> --body \"...\"`\n- **Apply / remove labels**: `gh issue edit <number> --add-label \"...\"` / `--remove-label \"...\"`\n- **Close**: `gh issue close <number> --comment \"...\"`\n\nInfer the repo from `git remote -v`; `gh` does this automatically when run inside a clone.\n\n## Pull requests as a triage surface\n\n**PRs as a request surface: no.** _(Set to `yes` if this repo treats external PRs as feature requests; `/triage` reads this flag.)_\n\nWhen set to `yes`, PRs run through the same labels and states as issues, using the `gh pr` equivalents:\n\n- **Read a PR**: `gh pr view <number> --comments` and `gh pr diff <number>` for the diff.\n- **List external PRs for triage**: `gh pr list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,author,authorAssociation,comments` then keep only `authorAssociation` of `CONTRIBUTOR`, `FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR`, or `NONE` (drop `OWNER`/`MEMBER`/`COLLABORATOR`).\n- **Comment / label / close**: `gh pr comment`, `gh pr edit --add-label`/`--remove-label`, `gh pr close`.\n\nGitHub shares one number space across issues and PRs, so a bare `#42` may be either: resolve with `gh pr view 42` and fall back to `gh issue view 42`.\n\n## When a skill says \"publish to the issue tracker\"\n\nCreate a GitHub issue. Apply a kind label at creation: `spec` for a spec issue (`to-spec`), `ticket` for a ticket issue (`to-tickets`). These are separate from the triage labels in `triage-labels.md`.\n\n## When a skill says \"fetch the relevant ticket\"\n\nRun `gh issue view <number> --comments`.\n\n## Wayfinding operations\n\nUsed by `/wayfinder`. The **map** is a single issue with **child** issues as tickets.\n\n- **Map**: a single issue labelled `wayfinder:map`, holding the Notes / Decisions-so-far / Fog body. `gh issue create --label wayfinder:map`.\n- **Child ticket**: an issue linked to the map as a GitHub sub-issue (`gh api` on the sub-issues endpoint). Where sub-issues aren't enabled, add the child to a task list in the map body and put `Part of #<map>` at the top of the child body. Labels: `wayfinder:<type>` (`research`/`prototype`/`grilling`/`task`). Once claimed, the ticket is assigned to the driving dev.\n- **Blocking**: GitHub's **native issue dependencies**, the canonical, UI-visible representation. Add an edge with `gh api --method POST repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<child>/dependencies/blocked_by -F issue_id=<blocker-db-id>`, where `<blocker-db-id>` is the blocker's numeric **database id** (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<n> --jq .id`, _not_ the `#number` or `node_id`). GitHub reports `issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by` (open blockers only, the live gate). Where dependencies aren't available, fall back to a `Blocked by: #<n>, #<n>` line at the top of the child body. A ticket is unblocked when every blocker is closed.\n- **Frontier query**: list the map's open children (`gh issue list --state open`, scoped to the map's sub-issues / task list), drop any with an open blocker (`issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by > 0`, or an open issue in the `Blocked by` line) or an assignee; first in map order wins.\n- **Claim**: `gh issue edit <n> --add-assignee @me`, the session's first write.\n- **Resolve**: `gh issue comment <n> --body \"<answer>\"`, then `gh issue close <n>`, then append a context pointer (gist + link) to the map's Decisions-so-far.\ndomain.md\nissue-tracker.md\ntriage-labels.md\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7d0a7fc2-3432-4ef7-91b2-0d0e0d9ae7ce","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:35:17.419Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      #
