os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\ntemplates:\nadmin\nbase.html\npublic\n\nscripts:\n__pycache__\ncheck_submission.py\nseed_demo.py\nusage_report.py\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.\n- **Comment on an issue**: `gh issue comment <number> --body \"...\"`\n- **Apply / remove labels**: `gh issue edit <number> --add-label \"...\"` / `--remove-label \"...\"`\n- **Close**: `gh issue close <number> --comment \"...\"`\n\nInfer the repo from `git remote -v`; `gh` does this automatically when run inside a clone.\n\n## Pull requests as a triage surface\n\n**PRs as a request surface: no.** _(Set to `yes` if this repo treats external PRs as feature requests; `/triage` reads this flag.)_\n\nWhen set to `yes`, PRs run through the same labels and states as issues, using the `gh pr` equivalents:\n\n- **Read a PR**: `gh pr view <number> --comments` and `gh pr diff <number>` for the diff.\n- **List external PRs for triage**: `gh pr list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,author,authorAssociation,comments` then keep only `authorAssociation` of `CONTRIBUTOR`, `FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR`, or `NONE` (drop `OWNER`/`MEMBER`/`COLLABORATOR`).\n- **Comment / label / close**: `gh pr comment`, `gh pr edit --add-label`/`--remove-label`, `gh pr close`.\n\nGitHub shares one number space across issues and PRs, so a bare `#42` may be either: resolve with `gh pr view 42` and fall back to `gh issue view 42`.\n\n## When a skill says \"publish to the issue tracker\"\n\nCreate a GitHub issue. Apply a kind label at creation: `spec` for a spec issue (`to-spec`), `ticket` for a ticket issue (`to-tickets`). These are separate from the triage labels in `triage-labels.md`.\n\n## When a skill says \"fetch the relevant ticket\"\n\nRun `gh issue view <number> --comments`.\n\n## Wayfinding operations\n\nUsed by `/wayfinder`. The **map** is a single issue with **child** issues as tickets.\n\n- **Map**: a single issue labelled `wayfinder:map`, holding the Notes / Decisions-so-far / Fog body. `gh issue create --label wayfinder:map`.\n- **Child ticket**: an issue linked to the map as a GitHub sub-issue (`gh api` on the sub-issues endpoint). Where sub-issues aren't enabled, add the child to a task list in the map body and put `Part of #<map>` at the top of the child body. Labels: `wayfinder:<type>` (`research`/`prototype`/`grilling`/`task`). Once claimed, the ticket is assigned to the driving dev.\n- **Blocking**: GitHub's **native issue dependencies**, the canonical, UI-visible representation. Add an edge with `gh api --method POST repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<child>/dependencies/blocked_by -F issue_id=<blocker-db-id>`, where `<blocker-db-id>` is the blocker's numeric **database id** (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<n> --jq .id`, _not_ the `#number` or `node_id`). GitHub reports `issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by` (open blockers only, the live gate). Where dependencies aren't available, fall back to a `Blocked by: #<n>, #<n>` line at the top of the child body. A ticket is unblocked when every blocker is closed.\n- **Frontier query**: list the map's open children (`gh issue list --state open`, scoped to the map's sub-issues / task list), drop any with an open blocker (`issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by > 0`, or an open issue in the `Blocked by` line) or an assignee; first in map order wins.\n- **Claim**: `gh issue edit <n> --add-assignee @me`, the session's first write.\n- **Resolve**: `gh issue comment <n> --body \"<answer>\"`, then `gh issue close <n>`, then append a context pointer (gist + link) to the map's Decisions-so-far.\ndomain.md\nissue-tracker.md\ntriage-labels.md\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"9a3220cf-633b-4cdd-acfe-789d7fdacec1","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6611344d-1a4c-43bc-8f36-1f73d29cbc50","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

:"to-spec","type":"assistant","uuid":"014ca72b-8b8c-4b20-a286-4d6553695ed8","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:36:21.188Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"014ca72b-8b8c-4b20-a286-4d6553695ed8","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYHwLuRy8nbbGHQGY7Rq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01155GEH5BJ5DL1JU1DA6t1R","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/spec.md","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As an Editor, I want to edit the Slug before first publish, so that I can shorten an awkward one.\n17. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1055,"cache_read_input_tokens":68798,"output_tokens":8550,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":1903},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1055,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":8550,"cache_read_input_tokens":68798,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1055,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1055},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01155GEH5BJ5DL1JU1DA6t1R":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/spec.md","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. {"parentUuid":"e816a8ee-eacf-45c6-bebf-c8c4cda2eb70","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"352c82c1-db7e-4925-b71d-47b823e7503e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01155GEH5BJ5DL1JU1DA6t1R","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/spec.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"b0908bbd-994c-4df8-b36d-2105af37f65f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:37:21.086Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/spec.md","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As an Editor, I want to edit the Slug before first publish, so that I can shorten an awkward one.\n17. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e816a8ee-eacf-45c6-bebf-c8c4cda2eb70","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2d19ce9f-3d97-435c-a7e9-eed655b4a2e6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYJ43NRts8PDEyBJirap","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The spec is published as **#1**: https://github.com/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/1, labelled `spec` and `ready-for-agent`.\n\nIt has 69 user stories and uses the `CONTEXT.md` vocabulary throughout. Tests go through the two seams you approved:\n- **Console:** `client` / `client_as` over HTTP, with a fresh database per test.\n- **Export:** `render_site(out, export_date=...)`, with assertions on the files it writes to `site/`.\n\nI settled the spec-level items you deferred. These are the ones most worth overriding if they don't fit the club:\n- **Slugs:** a clash in an auto-generated slug gets `-2`, `-3` added automatically. A slug typed by hand that clashes is an error. Slugs lock at first publish and stay locked even after Unpublish, so links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n- **Reserved Page slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus` and `style`, so a Page can't overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n- **Public dates:** News and Menus show when they were first published, and Events show their Event date. Both are in Spanish (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\") and in Lima time.\n- **Dashboard:** counts by kind and status, the time of the last Export, and \"N changes not yet Live\". Status filters stay visible even at zero, unlike the disappearing link in WordPress.\n- **First Admin:** created by the seed script from `.env`. There's no separate command.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News.\n- **Confirmation:** works without JavaScript. The first POST shows a confirm screen with a preview, and only a second POST applies the change.\n\n**Terminology:** the CLI keeps `cms publish` and `cms deploy` as separate commands. In the glossary's terms, running both is one Export.\n\n**What might grow scope:**\n- A Post's kind can't be changed after it's created.\n- Users can't change or reset their own passwords.\n\nBoth are listed as out of scope.\n\nNext, run `/clear` and then `/to-tickets` to cut #1 into vertical slices. Set `.claude/state/phase` to `tickets` when that starts."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":291,"cache_read_input_tokens":78530,"output_tokens":824,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":66},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":291,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":824,"cache_read_input_tokens":78530,"cache_creation_input_tokens":291,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":291},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"9289823a-909a-4190-a029-ec3b0b89c9d9","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYJ42xsnteHYNKceearM","attributionSkill":"to-spec","type":"assistant","uuid":"09fef892-840a-4893-91d2-1d407ed54eb8","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:37:36.299Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"47c8fd8a-23ba-4922-8a63-c3dee7927850","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5ff09920-982d-41cf-9f1f-703f1cdd7bf8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"<command-message>to-tickets</command-message>\n<command-name>/to-tickets</command-name>\n<command-args>Cut the spec into 8 tickets. {"parentUuid":"183721d7-a75a-4cd1-85e5-467dab5c1245","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"5ff09920-982d-41cf-9f1f-703f1cdd7bf8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/to-tickets\n\n# To Tickets\n\nBreak a plan, spec, or conversation into a set of **tickets**: tracer-bullet vertical slices, each declaring the tickets that **block** it.\n\nThe issue tracker and triage label vocabulary should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Gather context\n\nWork from whatever is already in the conversation context. If the user passes a reference (a spec path, an issue number or URL) as an argument, fetch it and read its full body and comments.\n\n### 2. Explore the codebase (optional)\n\nIf you have not already explored the codebase, do so to understand the current state of the code. Ticket titles and descriptions should use the project's domain glossary vocabulary, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\nLook for opportunities to prefactor the code to make the implementation easier. \"Make the change easy, then make the easy change.\"\n\n### 3. Draft vertical slices\n\nBreak the work into **tracer bullet** tickets.\n\n<vertical-slice-rules>\n\n- Each slice cuts a narrow but COMPLETE path through every layer (schema, API, UI, tests): vertical, NOT a horizontal slice of one layer\n- A completed slice is demoable or verifiable on its own\n- Each slice is sized to fit in a single fresh context window\n- Any prefactoring should be done first\n\n</vertical-slice-rules>\n\nGive each ticket its **blocking edges**: the other tickets that must complete before it can start. A ticket with no blockers can start immediately.\n\n**Wide refactors are the exception to vertical slicing.** A **wide refactor** is one mechanical change (rename a column, retype a shared symbol) whose **blast radius** fans across the whole codebase, so a single edit breaks thousands of call sites at once and no vertical slice can land green. Don't force it into a tracer bullet; sequence it as **expand–contract**. First expand: add the new form beside the old so nothing breaks. Then migrate the call sites over in batches sized by blast radius (per package, per directory), each batch its own ticket blocked by the expand, keeping CI green batch to batch because the old form still exists. Finally contract: delete the old form once no caller remains, in a ticket blocked by every migrate batch. When even the batches can't stay green alone, keep the sequence but let them share an integration branch that all block a final integrate-and-verify ticket; green is promised only there.\n\n### 4. Quiz the user\n\nPresent the proposed breakdown as a numbered list. For each ticket, show:\n\n- **Title**: short descriptive name\n- **Blocked by**: which other tickets (if any) must complete first\n- **What it delivers**: the end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work\n\nAsk the user:\n\n- Does the granularity feel right? (too coarse / too fine)\n- Are the blocking edges correct: does each ticket only depend on tickets that genuinely gate it?\n- Should any tickets be merged or split further?\n\nIterate until the user approves the breakdown.\n\n### 5. Publish the tickets to the configured tracker\n\nPublish the approved tickets. **How** depends on the tracker `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` configured; the tickets are the same either way, only the shape of the blocking edges changes:\n\n- **Local files** → write one file per ticket under `.scratch/<feature-slug>/issues/<NN>-<slug>.md`, numbered from `01` in dependency order (blockers first). Each file's \"Blocked by\" lists the numbers/titles it depends on. Use the per-ticket file template below: one ticket per file, never a single combined file.\n- **A real issue tracker (GitHub, Linear, …)** → publish one issue per ticket in dependency order (blockers first) so each ticket's blocking edges can reference real identifiers. Use the platform's native blocking / sub-issue relationship where it has one; otherwise set each ticket's \"Blocked by\" to the blocking issues. Apply the `ready-for-agent` triage label unless instructed otherwise; the tickets are agent-grabbable by construction.\n\nWork the **frontier**: any ticket whose blockers are all done. For a purely linear chain that means top to bottom.\n\nDo NOT close or modify any parent issue.\n\n<local-ticket-template>\n\n# <NN>: <Ticket title>\n\n**What to build:** the end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work, from the user's perspective, not a layer-by-layer implementation list.\n\n**Blocked by:** the numbers/titles of the tickets that gate this one, or \"None (can start immediately)\".\n\n**Status:** ready-for-agent\n\n- [ ] Acceptance criterion 1\n- [ ] Acceptance criterion 2\n\n</local-ticket-template>\n\n<issue-template>\n\n## Parent\n\nA reference to the parent issue on the tracker (if the source was an existing issue, otherwise omit this section).\n\n## What to build\n\nThe end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work, from the user's perspective, not layer-by-layer implementation.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Criterion 1\n- [ ] Criterion 2\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- A reference to each blocking ticket, or \"None (can start immediately)\".\n\n</issue-template>\n\nIn either form, avoid specific file paths or code snippets: they go stale fast. Exception: if a prototype produced a snippet that encodes a decision more precisely than prose can (state machine, reducer, schema, type shape), inline it and note briefly that it came from a prototype. Trim to the decision-rich parts, not a working demo, just the important bits.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Cut the spec into 8 tickets. Every ticket must have acceptance criteria and a \"Blocked by\" line, and be a thin vertical slice that can be demoed alone. Order them so T01 to T03 finish a working path end to end: T01 login and the two roles, T02 create and edit a Draft Post, T03 publish a Post and Export it to site/ so the public site goes live on GitHub Pages. Put Confirmation screens in their own ticket, and the seed script and dashboard near the end. Label every ticket \"ticket\"."}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"fc177bf8-9153-4399-b491-18289b374fdd","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:39:04.266Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

:"assistant","uuid":"410cf1a1-a32e-4b4d-8ea1-04b210ff2557","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:39:29.521Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"410cf1a1-a32e-4b4d-8ea1-04b210ff2557","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYJCVpsTS7EJdgouWgMT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've set the phase to `tickets`. Spec #1 has no comments, so I cut the tickets straight from its body. Here's the proposed breakdown. They'll be published as T01–T08, in dependency order.\n\n**1. T01: Login and the two roles.** Blocked by: none.\nAdds sign-in and sign-out with argon2 password hashes, a signed session, and CSRF on every state-changing form. A Deactivated User is refused at login and on their next request. Admin and Editor get genuinely different access: the Pages and Users console areas exist as placeholders, and an Editor gets 403 on them. Each test gets its own database. A minimal seed step creates the Admin and Editor from `.env`, so login can be demoed.\n\n**2. T02: Create and edit a Draft Post.** Blocked by: T01.\nAny Editor or Admin can create a News, Event or Menu Post as a Draft and edit it, including other people's Posts, while the original Author is kept. Slugs are generated from the title with accents stripped; an auto-generated clash gets `-2`, a typed clash is an error, and slugs can be edited before first publish. Preview shows the sanitized Markdown in the Spanish public layout. There's a basic Post list.\n\n**3. T03: Publish a Post and Export it (goes live on Pages).** Blocked by: T02.\nAdds publish and unpublish, without Confirmation yet. The Slug locks at first publish. `render_site(out, export_date)` writes only Published Posts: the Home page with the latest News, the `noticias/` feed and one page per Post. Everything is in Spanish with Lima dates, sanitized HTML, no Author names and relative paths only. Each Export is recorded. Done means the site is deployed and the live GitHub Pages URL loads.\n\n**4. T04: Events and the Menu on the public site.** Blocked by: T03.\nAdds Event date fields: a required start date, an optional time, and an optional end date that must be on or after the start. The Home page gets Upcoming Events (end date if set, otherwise start date, at or after the export date) and the `eventos/` feed shows Upcoming, then past. The Current menu goes on the Home page, and older Menus go in `menus/`, the Menu archive.\n\n**5. T05: Confirmation screens.** Blocked by: T03.\nPublishing, unpublishing, deleting (added here) and saving edits to Published content each go through a Confirmation screen with a preview. The first POST changes nothing, Cancel keeps the unsaved edits, and saving a Draft stays immediate. Deleting Published content counts toward the not-yet-Live count.\n\n**6. T06: Pages and Navigation (Admin only).** Blocked by: T05.\nAdmins get Page create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete, with Confirmation and a nav order. Reserved Slugs are rejected. The Export writes `<slug>.html` for each Page and builds the Navigation: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages in order. An Editor still gets 403.\n\n**7. T07: User management (Admin only).** Blocked by: T01.\nAdds a Users list with role and active status. An Admin can create a User, change a role, and deactivate or reactivate a User, but can't deactivate or demote themselves. A deactivated User's content keeps its Author.\n\n**8. T08: Seed demo content and the dashboard.** Blocked by: T04, T06.\nThe seed script creates demo content on a fresh clone: a Draft and a Published item of every kind, a weekend tournament and Pages. The dashboard shows counts by kind and status (plus Pages for Admins), the time of the last Export and \"N changes not yet Live\". The Post list gets kind and status filters that still show at zero.\n\nTwo notes on the edges:\n- **T07 depends only on T01**, so it could be done any time after login. It sits at 7 to follow your ordering.\n- **T06 is blocked by T05**, so that Pages reuse Confirmation instead of building their own. If you'd rather have Pages earlier, T06 could depend on T03 only, and T05 would then add Confirmation to both Posts and Pages."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1583,"cache_read_input_tokens":81938,"output_tokens":1654,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":17},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1583,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1654,"cache_read_input_tokens":81938,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1583,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1583},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"075b797e-1596-4d38-a019-59c076738df6","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfYJCV3EwP3FEnF35hvAZ","attributionSkill":"to-tickets","type":"assistant","uuid":"21c77825-d422-4dfb-8e92-bcb0fa2dd39a","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:39:35.710Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
