{"parentUuid":"e35ba8de-64c8-40d5-bd9b-16a2c7b79b59","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYJHtMqefGPjMbWE2U4z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UgqZ4a2k3spXWo8ukocqTY","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"mkdir -p /tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/tickets && cd $_ && \ncat > T01.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nOffice staff can sign in to the console and sign out. There are two roles with genuinely different access. An Admin reaches every console area. An Editor is refused, server-side, from the Pages and Users areas, which exist as Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07 fill them in. A Deactivated User can't sign in, and a signed-in User who gets deactivated loses access on their next request. Every state-changing form carries a CSRF token.\n\nThe seed step creates the first Admin and Editor from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` / `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`, so login can be demoed on a fresh clone. T08 adds demo content later. Each test gets its own fresh database seeded with the demo Admin and Editor, so the existing `client_as` fixture works.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] `client_as(\"admin\")` and `client_as(\"editor\")` sign in successfully, and the `client_as` skip in the conftest file no longer triggers.\n- [ ] A wrong email and a wrong password show the same generic error.\n- [ ] Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes, and no password appears in logs or responses.\n- [ ] Signing out ends the session; a later console request redirects to login.\n- [ ] Every console address redirects a signed-out visitor to login.\n- [ ] A Deactivated User can't sign in, and an already signed-in User who is deactivated is refused on their next request.\n- [ ] An Editor gets 403 on the Pages and Users console areas, even when typing the address directly. An Admin gets 200.\n- [ ] A state-changing POST without a valid CSRF token is rejected (login and logout included).\n- [ ] The seed step creates the Admin and Editor from environment variables and refuses to run without them.\n- [ ] Each test runs against its own temporary database; `create_app()` accepts a database path.\n- [ ] The T00 tests still pass.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- None (can start immediately)\nEOF\ncat > T02.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nA signed-in Editor or Admin can create a Post of kind News, Event or Menu, write its body in Markdown and save it as a Draft. They can reopen and edit any Post, including one written by someone else, and the Author stays the original writer. The Slug is generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`) and can be edited. Preview shows the Post as Members will see it: the Spanish public layout with sanitized Markdown. A basic Post list shows title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time.\n\nEvent date fields come in T04. Here, an Event is just a kind.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Editor can create a Post of each kind; it's saved as a Draft with the Editor as Author.\n- [ ] A Post's kind can't be changed after creation.\n- [ ] The Slug is generated from the title in lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words.\n- [ ] An auto-generated Slug that clashes within the same kind gets `-2`, `-3`, … appended.\n- [ ] A Slug typed by hand that clashes within the same kind is a form error, and nothing is saved.\n- [ ] Any Editor can edit a Post written by another Editor, and the Author doesn't change.\n- [ ] Preview renders the Markdown sanitized: a `<script>` in the body is stripped, and an `http(s)` image link survives.\n- [ ] Preview uses the Spanish public layout and shows no Author name.\n- [ ] The Post list shows every Post with title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in America/Lima.\n- [ ] Every form carries the CSRF token.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T01}\nEOF\ncat > T03.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nAn Editor can publish a Draft Post and unpublish a Published one. Confirmation screens come in T05. When the developer runs an Export (`cms publish` then `cms deploy`), only Published Posts are written to `site/`, and the public site goes live on GitHub Pages. The Home page shows the latest 5 News, `noticias/` lists all News, and every Published Post gets its own page. The site is in Spanish, dates are in Lima time, and no Author names appear. Markdown is sanitized and every link and asset path is relative. Each Export is recorded.\n\nThis completes the first end-to-end path: login → write → publish → Export → live site.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Editor can publish a Draft Post and unpublish a Published one. Unpublishing returns the Post to Draft.\n- [ ] Publishing sets the first-published time, which never changes afterwards. The Slug can't be edited once a Post has been published, even after Unpublishing.\n- [ ] `render_site(out, export_date=...)` writes only Published Posts; a Draft never appears as a page, feed entry or Home page item.\n- [ ] The Home page shows the latest 5 Published News, newest first; `noticias/index.html` lists all Published News.\n- [ ] Each Published Post has its own page under its kind's folder.\n- [ ] News dates show the first-published date in Spanish, in America/Lima (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\").\n- [ ] No Author name appears anywhere in `site/`.\n- [ ] A `<script>` in a body is stripped in `site/`.\n- [ ] No `href=\"/` or `src=\"/` appears in any file under `site/`.\n- [ ] Each Export records the time it ran.\n- [ ] The site is deployed with `cms deploy`, the GitHub Pages URL loads with the stylesheet, and the URL is recorded in the README.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T02}\nEOF\ncat > T04.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nEvents get an Event date: a required start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. The public site answers \"when is the next event?\": the Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events, and `eventos/` lists Upcoming Events soonest first, then past Events most recent first. The Event date is shown first on every Event.\n\nThe Menu gets its public shape too. The Home page shows only the Current menu, meaning the Published Menu with the latest first-published time. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive in `menus/`, newest first. Nobody has to unpublish last week's Menu.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Saving an Event without a start date is a form error.\n- [ ] Saving an Event whose end date is earlier than its start date is a form error with a clear message.\n- [ ] Event date fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- [ ] An Event is Upcoming when its end date, or its start date if it has no end date, is on or after the export date.\n- [ ] A Saturday–Sunday tournament is Upcoming when exported on its Sunday and not Upcoming when exported on Monday.\n- [ ] The Home page shows at most 3 Upcoming Events, soonest first.\n- [ ] `eventos/index.html` shows Upcoming Events soonest first, then past Events most recent first.\n- [ ] Each Event shows its date first, in Spanish, with the time when set, in Lima time.\n- [ ] The Home page shows only the Current menu; publishing a newer Menu moves the previous one to the Menu archive at the next Export.\n- [ ] `menus/index.html` lists every Published Menu except the Current menu, newest first.\n- [ ] Draft Events and Menus never appear anywhere in `site/`.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T03}\nEOF\ncat > T05.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nEvery change to what Members will see goes through a Confirmation screen, which is the club's main defence against a wrong price. The screen appears before publishing, unpublishing, deleting (added in this ticket) and saving edits to Published content. It summarises the change and, where relevant, shows the rendered preview. Only a second, confirmed submission applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the unsaved values intact. Saving a Draft stays instant. It uses no JavaScript.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Publishing a Post first shows a Confirmation screen with the preview; the Post stays a Draft until the confirmed submission.\n- [ ] Saving edits to a Published Post first shows a Confirmation screen; the stored Post is unchanged until confirmed.\n- [ ] Unpublishing needs Confirmation.\n- [ ] Deleting a Post needs Confirmation that says deletion is permanent; after confirming, the Post is gone.\n- [ ] Cancel on any Confirmation screen returns to the editor with the unsaved values still filled in.\n- [ ] Saving a Draft needs no Confirmation.\n- [ ] A confirmed submission without a valid CSRF token is rejected.\n- [ ] Deleting a Published Post is counted as a change not yet Live until the next Export.\n- [ ] The public site has no JavaScript.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T03}\nEOF\ncat > T06.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nAdmins manage Pages: standing content such as rules, membership and about. They can create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages, with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, and give each Page a navigation order number. The Export writes each Published Page to the site root and builds the Navigation on every public page: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages in the Admin's order. Editors are still refused.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Admin can create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete a Page. Publishing, unpublishing, deleting and saving a Published Page each need Confirmation.\n- [ ] An Editor gets 403 on every Page route, including a direct POST.\n- [ ] A Page Slug of `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus` or `style` is rejected.\n- [ ] The Page Slug follows the same generation, clash and lock-at-first-publish rules as Posts.\n- [ ] Each Published Page is written to the site root; a Draft Page never appears in `site/` or the Navigation.\n- [ ] Every public page shows the Navigation in this order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Published Pages by nav order, with ties broken by title.\n- [ ] All Navigation links are relative and work from nested pages (for example, from inside `noticias/`).\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T05}\nEOF\ncat > T07.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nAdmins manage Users. The Users area lists every User with their role and whether they're active. An Admin can create a User (name, email, role, initial password), change a User's role, and deactivate or reactivate a User. An Admin can't deactivate or demote themselves, so the club always has an Admin. Users are never deleted, so every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Admin sees all Users with name, email, role and active status.\n- [ ] An Admin can create a User; the new User can sign in with the initial password, which is stored only as an argon2 hash.\n- [ ] Creating a User with an email already in use (case-insensitive) is a form error.\n- [ ] An Admin can change another User's role, and the new permissions apply on that User's next request.\n- [ ] An Admin can deactivate another User, who then can't sign in; reactivating restores access.\n- [ ] An Admin trying to deactivate or demote themselves is refused with an explanation.\n- [ ] A Deactivated User's Posts keep their status and their Author.\n- [ ] There is no way to delete a User.\n- [ ] An Editor gets 403 on every Users route, including a direct POST.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T01}\nEOF\ncat > T08.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nA grader or new office member can try the CMS right away, and staff can see the state of the site at a glance. The seed script fills a fresh database with demo content: a Draft and a Published item of every Post kind, a Saturday–Sunday tournament Event, a weekly Menu and some Pages. The dashboard shows counts of Posts by kind and status (and Pages by status for Admins), the time of the last Export and how many changes are not yet Live. The Post list gains kind and status filters that stay visible even when they match nothing.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] On a fresh clone with the `.env.example` values, the seed script runs cleanly and creates the Admin, the Editor, and a Draft and a Published Post of each kind, including a multi-day Event, plus at least one Draft and one Published Page.\n- [ ] Running the seed script twice doesn't duplicate Users or content.\n- [ ] The dashboard shows Post counts by kind × status for every User, and Page counts by status for Admins only.\n- [ ] The dashboard shows the time of the last Export in Lima time, or \"never\".\n- [ ] The dashboard shows how many changes are not yet Live: Posts and Pages updated since the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. With no Export yet, every Published item counts.\n- [ ] After an Export, the not-yet-Live count is 0.\n- [ ] The Post list can be filtered by kind and by status; filters that match nothing are still shown, with a count of 0.\n- [ ] `CMS_SITE_TITLE` in `.env.example` is the club's name.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T04}\n- {T06}\nEOF\nls","description":"Write the eight ticket bodies to scratchpad"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1754,"cache_read_input_tokens":83521,"output_tokens":4945,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":119},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1754,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":4945,"cache_read_input_tokens":83521,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1754,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1754},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01UgqZ4a2k3spXWo8ukocqTY":{"command":"mkdir -p /tmp/claude-1000/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64/scratchpad/tickets && cd $_ && \ncat > T01.md <<'EOF'\n## Parent\n\n#1\n\n## What to build\n\nOffice staff can sign in to the console and sign out. The dashboard shows counts of Posts by kind and status (and Pages by status for Admins), the time of the last Export and how many changes are not yet Live. The Post list gains kind and status filters that stay visible even when they match nothing.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] On a fresh clone with the `.env.example` values, the seed script runs cleanly and creates the Admin, the Editor, and a Draft and a Published Post of each kind, including a multi-day Event, plus at least one Draft and one Published Page.\n- [ ] Running the seed script twice doesn't duplicate Users or content.\n- [ ] The dashboard shows Post counts by kind × status for every User, and Page counts by status for Admins only.\n- [ ] The dashboard shows the time of the last Export in Lima time, or \"never\".\n- [ ] The dashboard shows how many changes are not yet Live: Posts and Pages updated since the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. With no Export yet, every Published item counts.\n- [ ] After an Export, the not-yet-Live count is 0.\n- [ ] The Post list can be filtered by kind and by status; filters that match nothing are still shown, with a count of 0.\n- [ ] `CMS_SITE_TITLE` in `.env.example` is the club's name.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- {T04}\n- {T06}\nEOF\nls","description":"Write the eight ticket bodies to scratchpad"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"2e5acf32-a34e-44c5-845c-194d14d9f142","apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfYJHsvZ7BiKqtgJ1wxMp","attributionSkill":"to-tickets","type":"assistant","uuid":"8b497f03-999b-4c33-910e-39cedbbea109","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:41:17.497Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6cbe0ce0-e846-43a4-ae88-10cec5924f64","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
